A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45     100.0%      100.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      66.7%       66.7% 
       >0.35     367      88.9%       88.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          78.6%         55.6%      8.8%        6.7%   -5.419   109.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      20.0%       25.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      66.7%       66.7% 
       >0.35     367      90.3%       90.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          81.8%         38.2%     10.7%        0.0%   -5.281   107.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      66.7%       60.0% 
       >0.35     367      93.3%       93.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          82.6%         37.0%      9.0%        0.0%   -5.397   109.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45      40.0%       25.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      92.3%       92.1% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45     100.0%      100.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      44.4%       44.4% 
       >0.35     367      88.2%       88.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          68.0%         54.8%     20.3%        1.0%   -4.671    96.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      20.0%       20.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      36.4%       45.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      30.8%       30.8% 
       >0.35     367      81.8%       81.1% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      16.7%       16.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      58.3%       58.3% 
       >0.35     367      86.6%       87.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      33.3%       33.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      41.2%       41.2% 
       >0.35     367      82.8%       83.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       28.6% 
   0.05-0.15      45      33.3%       27.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      45.0%       47.4% 
       >0.35     367      79.4%       79.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.0%         47.7%     25.4%        1.5%   -4.331    90.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      28.6%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      30.0%       30.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      63.6%       63.6% 
       >0.35     367      84.8%       84.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.8%         50.0%     24.6%        2.4%   -4.384    91.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      20.0%       16.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      44.4%       44.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      60.0%       52.9% 
       >0.35     367      84.9%       85.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          67.3%         57.7%     21.7%        0.9%   -4.586    94.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      16.7%       16.7% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      41.2%       38.9% 
       >0.35     367      81.0%       79.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.8%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      36.8%       29.4% 
       >0.35     367      86.3%       85.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.3%         48.2%     27.5%        0.7%   -4.186    87.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      41.2%       37.5% 
       >0.35     367      87.7%       87.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          79.5%         43.8%     25.0%        0.8%   -4.347    90.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      54.5%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      54.5%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      86.3%       87.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      57.1%       57.1% 
       >0.35     367      87.0%       86.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.8%       53.8% 
   0.15-0.35      68      41.2%       38.9% 
       >0.35     367      86.7%       87.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      86.6%       88.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          77.5%         48.0%     33.8%        2.3%   -3.776    80.7

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      56.2%       61.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      45.5%       42.1% 
       >0.35     367      87.9%       87.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          72.3%         51.3%     29.7%        2.6%   -4.050    85.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      38.1%       38.1% 
       >0.35     367      84.9%       85.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.4%         51.9%     41.8%        3.3%   -3.265    71.5

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      22.2%       22.2% 
   0.05-0.15      45      35.7%       33.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      51.6%       53.1% 
       >0.35     367      85.5%       86.1% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          77.6%         43.6%     46.1%        1.7%   -2.979    66.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      40.0%       42.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      48.5%       51.5% 
       >0.35     367      87.8%       87.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          78.2%         45.1%     58.0%        2.7%   -2.212    53.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      33.3%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      62.5%       62.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      39.5%       41.0% 
       >0.35     367      88.2%       88.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          77.3%         44.5%     58.4%        1.3%   -2.190    52.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      33.3%       33.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      54.2%       52.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      48.7%       48.7% 
       >0.35     367      86.3%       86.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      22.2%       22.2% 
   0.05-0.15      45      64.3%       64.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      56.8%       55.6% 
       >0.35     367      88.4%       87.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      51.7%       51.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      47.6%       46.3% 
       >0.35     367      88.3%       87.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          80.5%         44.5%     67.2%        2.9%   -1.636    43.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      60.0%       60.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      57.4%       58.3% 
       >0.35     367      90.5%       90.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          78.7%         44.4%     69.5%        2.5%   -1.491    40.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      51.6%       51.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      57.4%       57.4% 
       >0.35     367      88.0%       88.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.3%       48.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      54.0%       56.0% 
       >0.35     367      89.1%       89.1% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          79.3%         44.2%     72.9%        2.9%   -1.282    37.0

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      23.1%       23.1% 
   0.05-0.15      45      51.4%       51.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      57.4%       57.4% 
       >0.35     367      89.7%       89.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          78.6%         44.1%     74.4%        2.9%   -1.185    35.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       21.4% 
   0.05-0.15      45      58.3%       58.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      52.1%       52.1% 
       >0.35     367      88.9%       89.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          78.7%         42.0%     75.4%        2.6%   -1.112    34.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       46.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      56.9%       52.8% 
       >0.35     367      90.0%       89.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          82.0%         39.6%     75.4%        2.3%   -1.104    34.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      33.3%       36.4% 
   0.05-0.15      45      57.1%       57.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      55.3%       56.5% 
       >0.35     367      90.3%       90.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

observation shows the network has a receptive field, which it has
before any training at all.
